UART COMMANDS: 
--------------------------------------------------
T: Toggle control mode between auto and manual
M: MAP
C: Move within 5cm of object directly in front
W: Forward
A: Left
S: Backward
D: Right
Q: GET CURRENT POSITION
R: RETURN TO START POINT
E: EXIT

CprE288 Simple Mission



UART COMMANDS: 
--------------------------------------------------
T: Toggle control mode between auto and manual
M: MAP
C: Move within 5cm of object directly in front
W: Forward
A: Left
S: Backward
D: Right
Q: GET CURRENT POSITION
R: RETURN TO START POINT
E: EXIT
